By high-dimensional geometry arguments, we define the map iteratively: at each stage n, the compatibility condition is ensured by the universal property of the ambient category. The resulting object satisfies all axioms by construction, and its uniqueness follows from the terminality argument. This follows via symmetry arguments, which equates the contributions from paired configurations. ∎
